營收走勢圖

研究員/ 鄭高輯 owencheng@uni-psg.com

結論: 
維持聯電買進建議，理由包括: (1) 2Q26 毛利率及 EPS 表現優於
預期。(2) 終端市場不佳的背景下，3Q26 毛利率及稼動率表現仍
強勁，市佔率持續提升。(3)AI 已經貢獻今年 3 億美元營收，未來
三年超過 10 億美元。(4) 12 吋 PIC 已開始量產，TFLN 調變器也
將是首家量產的公司。(5) 先進封裝包括 DTC 中介層、3D 堆疊，
將於 2026 年~2027 年初開始貢獻。(6) 上調 2026 年資本支出至
20 億美元，擴充矽光子及先進封裝產能。預估2026、2027 年稅後
EPS 分別為 7.27 元(業外貢獻 2.5 元)及 7.33 元，考量價格環境
改善 ， 且矽光及先進封裝為新動能 ， 給予PER 20X， 目標價146 元，
維持買進建議。

重點摘要: 
1.2Q26 毛利率及 EPS 表現優於預期:  
聯電 2Q26 營收 12.6%QoQ(+17.0%YoY)，主要係受惠於通訊及消費
性電子的強勁需求 ， 出貨量+10.6%QoQ 至 112.9 萬片 ， 優於財測的
+高個位數 QoQ 成長；ASP 提升低個位數%，符合財測。在毛利率方
面，較前季提升3.3pt 至 32.5%，優於財測的「接近30%」，主要
係 UTR 由 79%提升至 85%，優於財測的 81~83%。業外認列 302.36
億 ， 主要係投資收益 。 單季稅後EPS 3.39 元 ， 大幅優於預期的1.38
元。本季22/28nm 需求持續強勁，比重由34%提升至 37%，再創新
高。

2.終端市場不佳的背景下，3Q26 毛利率及稼動率表現仍強勁:  
展望 3Q26，PC、通訊與消費性電子需求持續穩健，且 8 吋受惠於
PMIC、MCU 強勁需求，帶動UTR 大幅提升。在財測方面，預估出貨
量成長高個位數%QoQ，ASP 維持穩健，毛利率提升至 34~36%，產
能利用率進一步提升至 90%以上。

儘管 AI 需求強勁，但非 AI 領域復甦並非全面復甦，公司觀察智
慧型手機和消費的庫存和 DOI 同步上升，消費支出疲軟讓短期手
機及 PC 出貨 YoY 下滑，另汽車工業領域的需求雖穩定，但DOI 仍
高於歷史平均，因此將謹慎觀察庫存變化。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

儘管如此 ， 聯電特殊製程佔比達50%以上 ， 且公司持續在晶圓代工
增加市佔率，同時客戶在 AI 和非 AI 市場市佔率也在增加，因此
公司預計第三季整體 UTR 將大於 90%，12 吋高於公司平均，而 8
吋 UTR 也有 85%。

3.上調資本支出至 20 億美元，擴充矽光子及先進封裝產能:  
公司將 2026 年資本支出由 15 億美元上調至 20 億美元，主要用於
新加坡 12i P4 及台南 12A P7/8 擴產。其中，新加坡 P4 廠無塵
室是為了矽光子產能而準備；台南新建的晶圓廠是為先進封裝而
準備，由於建設時間拉長，預估會有大於20 個月的準備時間，因
此量產預計到 2028~2029 年。此外，預算增加有一部份為新加坡
P3 產能擴充，將增加 BCD 以及矽光子產能，另台南也將投入客製
化記憶體堆疊(CMS)和 DTC，同時在 8 吋建立薄膜鈮酸鋰(TFLN)產
能。此外，董事會會議已核准將近50 億美元資本支出，用於未來
2~3 年，同時折舊曲線將改變，預估 2026、2027 年折舊金額增速
將會是每年增加低十位數。

4.AI 已經貢獻今年 3 億美元營收，未來三年超過 10 億美元:  
AI 相關已開始成為公司 2026 年成長的重要動能，相關產品包括: 
PMIC、通訊(connectivity)、FPGA、先進封裝和矽光子。公司預估
2026 年AI 營收將近 3 億美元(預估佔比約 3%)，未來三年預計將
超過 10 億美元(預估佔比約高個位數%)。

5. 先進封裝包括 DTC 中介層、3D 堆疊，將於 2026 年~2027 年初
開始貢獻:  
在先進封裝方面， 公司預期到2030 年可觸及的市場將成長一倍以
上。目前技術已擴展到獨立深槽電容(DTC)的 2.5D 中介層、3D 晶
圓對晶圓堆疊(RF SOI) 以及記憶體對記憶體堆疊技術。目前已有
10 多個活躍客戶 ， 並有超過35 個新產品正在討論中 ， 預計在2026
年~2027 年初開始貢獻。

6. 12 吋 PIC 已開始量產，TFLN 調變器也將是首家量產的公司 
公司本月已將首批 12 吋矽光子 PIC 量產晶圓交付客戶，2027 年
將進一步推出可供更多客戶導入之矽光子平台，12 吋 PIC 提供更
好的製程控制和效能，跟競爭對手 8 吋做出區隔。至於薄膜鈮酸
鋰(TFLN)， 公司已經將是全球上第一個將TFLN 調變器投入生產的
公司，這是傳輸速度超過 400G 的最佳解決方案。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

7.2027 年價格有機會更好，且光通及先進封裝為新動能，預估稅
後 EPS 7.33 元:  
展望 2027 年，就價格而言，公司認為受惠AI 和非 AI 相關需求推
動 ， 訂價環境變得更具有支撐 ， 預計今年訂價趨勢會比預期好 ， 並
預期在 2027 年會有更有意義的價格上漲 。 此外 ，AI 相關應用已經
成為公司新的成長動能，光通相關的 PIC 及 TFLN，以及先進封裝
(DTC 中介層、3D 堆疊)等逐步放量，加上公司配合客戶產能擴充，
可望延續 2026 年成長重要成長動能。研究員預估 2027 年營收
+22.8%YoY，稅後 EPS 7.33 元(原估 5.87 元)。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

## Document 060: 2308台達電 宏遠

Source PDF: all report pdf/2308台達電 宏遠.pdf
Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1

2308 台達電(Delta)

報告日期： 2026/07/31 研 究 員：吳和善  (Jason Wu) 
壹、投 資 建 議 
一. 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 9.68 元，YoY+80% 
2Q26 合併營收為 1,832 億元，YoY+48%、QoQ+15%，其中電源及
零組件營收年增 50%、占比達 54%。34%的基礎設施年增 78%，其
動能主要受惠於散熱基期較低、2Q26 貢獻已達 10% 。2Q26 毛利率
為 35.6% 、 費用率略低於 19% ， 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 9.68 元 ， YoY+80% 。

二. AI 電源與散熱將持續推升 2026 年營運動能 
現階段公司 AI 電源營收已超過 25%、散熱液冷產品達 10%，預期
2H26 仍維持強勁營運動能。展望 2H26 ，電源年成長相對高於由於
液冷產品；HVDC 部分，3Q26 將有 小量出貨，其中由於 400VDC
為直接對 CSP 出貨因此量相對較多，但整體明年才有顯著放量。

三. 調整供貨及成本結構亦持續擴增產能因應 
2026 年起原物料及相關電子零組件皆呈現漲價情況 ， 從 2Q26 開始
有受到成本上漲影響，因此開始針對合約與客戶進行協商預期有
望在 1~2 季度陸續開始反應售價以保有合理毛利率；而在資本支
出部分，2025 年資本支出達 466 億元，預期全年將達 700 億元。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 為 39.57 元，YoY+71% 
預估 2026 年營收將達 7,603 億元，YoY+37% 。由於 2Q26 毛利不如
預期，考量將受成本提升影響抵銷部分 2H26 營運規模效益，故有
下修全年毛利率至 36.13%，費用持續因擴大投資、整體費用率降
至 17.95%，上修 2026 年全年稅後 EPS 為 39.57 元，YoY+71%。

五. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 為 65.61 元，YoY+60% 
預估 2027 年營收為 1.082 兆元，YoY+42%，毛利率略降至 36.1%。
由於公司持續挹注資本投入故有上修費用支出但因經濟規模使得
費用率降至 16%、營利率則升至 20%，2027 年稅後 EPS 為 65.61
元，YoY+60%。

六. 維持買進，目標價 2,300 元

業務後已提升至解決方案整合商，投資建議維持買進，惟美國 Fed
升息機率提高將不利評價故有下修本益比，目標價為 2,300 元。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
台達電 (2308) 成立於創立於  1971 年 ， 過往台達電專注於IT 相關的
電源供應器及風扇，包括 NB、DT、Monitor 、Server 、Printer 及網通
類產品，因全球電子產業的轉變，及環保意識的抬頭，台達電近
年積極跨入工業自動化、智慧安防及綠能領域。近年來，台達集
團已逐步從關鍵零組件製造商邁入整體節能解決方案提供者，深
耕「電源及零組件」 、「自動化」與「基礎設施」三大業務範疇，
為全球提供電源管理與散熱解決方案的轉業廠商，在面對日益嚴
重的氣候變遷，公司秉持「環保  節能  愛地球」的經營使命，持
續開發創新節能產品及解決方案、致力於提升產品的能源轉換效
率，減輕全球暖化對人類生存的衝擊。

圖一、台達電解決方案產品

資料來源：台達電

二. 公司營運概況與展望 
1.2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 9.68 元，YoY+80% 
2Q26 合併營收為 1,832 億元，YoY+48%、QoQ+15%，成長動能主
要來自於 AI 強勁需求帶動。產品結構部分，電源及零組件營收年
增 50%、占比達 54%，其中主要貢獻來自於伺服器電源。營收占
比達 34%的基礎設施年增 78% ， 其動能主要受惠於散熱基期較低、
2Q26 貢獻已達 10%。其他交通及自動化業務動能較為疲弱且呈現
小幅虧損狀態但不影響整體營運趨勢。2Q26 毛利率為 35.6%低於
1Q26 水準(1Q26 毛利率較高主因一次性銷貨補貼收入及認列條款
影響)部分略受到零件成本影響。費用率略低於 19%，主要因擴大
營運量能提高 RD 費用等，2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 9.68 元，YoY+80%。

2.AI 電源與散熱將持續推升 2026 年營運動能 
雖然近期美國 AI 大廠面臨資本支出及現金流等質疑，不過公司認
為 AI 應用仍持續普及，再加上觀察客戶端資本支出持續成長趨
勢，故認為 AI 建置潮仍將持續，惟過程中仍將持續面臨零組件供
貨、資料中心選址及施工遞延等狀況。以現階段公司 AI 電源營收
已超過 25%、散熱液冷產品達 10%，預期 2H26 仍維持強勁營運動
能。展望 2H26，電源年成長相對高於由於液冷產品(2H25 基期較
高) ； HVDC 部分 ， 公司認為 400VDC 、 800VDC 維持進度 ， 預計 3Q26
起將有小量出貨，其中由於 400VDC 為直接對 CSP 出貨因此量相
對較多，800VDC 因將由 NV 銷售客戶因此仍有待確認上市後狀
況，但整體而言 HVDC 今年僅有小量出貨，明年才有顯著放量。

3.調整供貨及成本結構亦持續擴增產能因應 
2026 年起原物料及相關電子零組件皆呈現漲價情況，公司持續藉
由規模優勢確保料源 ，雖然公司在記憶體使用較少但從 2Q26 開始
有受到成本上漲影響，因此開始針對合約與客戶進行協商預期有
望在 1~2 季度陸續開始反應售價以保有合理毛利率；而在資本支
出部分，2025 年資本支出達 466 億元，1H26 支出達 275 億元，預
期全年將達 700 億元。工廠部分，公司將更新泰國一廠擴增數倍
產能、2027 年再有 3 新廠完工，另仍將在台灣、中國及美國擴增
廠房大樓及 RD 人員以確保未來可跟上客戶 AI 及能源相關需求。

二. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 為 65.61 元，YoY+60% 
隨新一代伺服器上市 2027 年放量帶動電源規格及需求再度提升，
而 HVDC 於 2027 年被納入 AI 資料中心建置後開始放量，由於公
司將以 Power Rack 出整合出貨將放大營收規模，且帶動台達電競
爭優勢，由於公司已有 HVDC 產品再加上整合市電經由資料中心
至 AI 伺服器的應用因此營收規模仍可維持強勁動能，預估 2027
年營收為 1.082 兆元，YoY+42%，毛利率略降至 36.1%。由於公司
持續挹注資本投入故有上修費用支出但因經濟規模使得費用率降
至 16% 、 營利率則升至 20% ， 2027 年稅後 EPS 為 65.61 元 ， YoY+60% 。

國巨* (2327 TT) 
營運逐季向上趨勢明確

永豐觀點 
B/B Ratio 逐月往上，2026/6 月 B/B Ratio 為 2.2，且越來越多客戶願意溢價確保
供貨順暢。另一方面，AI 佔比也提升至 16%。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議：主係看好國巨營運將受惠於 (1) 日韓廠商將低階 MLCC 產能
移轉至高階產品的訂單外溢效應，(2) 管理層預估 3Q26 稼動率提升至 90%。另
一方面，下半年漲價效應浮現將進一步推升毛利率表現，(3) 除 MLCC 外，鉭質電
容、電感及晶片電阻的用量增加。綜上所述，考量國巨受惠轉單效應、被動元件產
業循環向上、仍有持續漲價機會，以 2027-2028 年平均 EPS 預估，目標價 1240
元(30 X 2027-28F 平均 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
國巨企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

2

國巨集團為台灣被動元件第一大供應商：2Q26 營收產品別占比 MLCC 19.1%，
Resistor 14.3%，T antalum(Kemet) 23.8%，Magnetics 23.5%，Sensor 10.9%，
Other Components 8.4%，其中 MLCC 與電阻成長主要來自標準品與特殊品需求
強勁 ； 終端應用占比 ：Industrial 28.4%，Computing & Enterprise System 27.3%，
Automotive 15.9%，Consumer 12.2%，T elecom 12.2%，Aerospace/Defense 
/Medical 4%，其中 AI 佔比約為 16%，較上季成長一個百分點。 
產品漲價及稼動率提升 ，2Q26 毛利率 38.5%：國巨於第二季針對部分產品價格調
漲 20-40%不等，除因原物料價格上升外，因 AI 伺服器使部分高階產品需求明顯
提升。整體而言，所有產品線、區域及終端應用領域皆為成長。國巨 2Q26 營收
444.56 億元 (+16.5% QoQ ， +35.7% YoY) ， 在 稼 動 率 提 升 下 ， 毛 利 率
38.5%(+0.4ppt QoQ，+2.9ppt YoY)， 營利率26.4%(+1.2pptQoQ，+4.9pptYoY)，
稅後淨利 94.18 億元(+17.7%QoQ，+88.4%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 4.59 元，因業外
認列未分配盈餘稅，影響 EPS 約-0.22 元，使 EPS 不及預期的 4.9 元。 
日韓產能溢出，國巨將直接受惠：雖相較於日韓國際大廠，國巨高階 MLCC 產品
市占率約為 10-20%，然因 Nvidia 下一世代 Vera Rubin 平台 MLCC 用量將較
Blackwell 平台近翻倍成長，且高階 MLCC 產品所耗用產能為中低階 MLCC 產品
的 8-10 倍。此外，因日韓廠商高階MLCC 產能滿載，故預期國巨將受惠於日韓廠
商將低階 MLCC 產能移轉至高階產品的訂單外溢效應，使得國巨在中低階 MLCC
訂價權將有更大的市場主導能力，營運將明顯受惠此效應。 
B/B Ratio 高達 2.2，3Q26 營運樂觀：展望第三季，受惠需求持續增加，除 B/B 
Ratio 不斷上升外，越來越多客戶對 LTA表現興趣，願支付溢價確保供應無虞。另
一方面，3Q26 稼動率持續提升，標準品及高階特殊品皆將提升至90%，上季分別
為 80-85%及 85-90%。在受惠於稼動率持續提升及漲價效應顯現下，預估國巨
3Q26 營收 490.7 億元(+10.4% QoQ，+48.3% YoY)， 毛利率40%(+1.5ppt QoQ，
+3.8ppt YoY)，營利率 27.2%(+0.8pptQoQ，+4.3pptYoY)，稅後淨利 112.45 億
元(+19.4%QoQ，+76.9%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 5.48 元。 
2Q26 各通路經銷商庫存健康：因預期未來市場在伺服器及車用需求增加，供需缺
口擴大使得產品將漲價， 供應商或通路商將提前進貨並控制庫存水位； 觀察國巨在
2017-19 年存貨狀況，國巨在 4Q17 起存貨明顯增加並延續 5 季至 1Q19 存貨開
始下降，營收高點在 3Q18，存貨高點則於 4Q18。觀察目前狀況，2Q26 無論在
大中華及全球通路商，和高服務(線上)通路皆較去年同期降低。 
產業循環向上空間可期：在 AI 伺服器帶動下，被動元件用量明顯增加，進一步帶
動日韓廠商發出漲價通知，雖國巨未能直接受惠於高階 MLCC 產品用量的增加，
然因轉單效應的顯現，仍使國巨中低階產品具有市場主導權。此外，國巨近年併購
轉型有成，產品多元，產業應用廣泛，獲利趨於穩定成長。目前以 AI 相關終端需
求最為強勁，國巨產品亦將受惠鉭質電容、電感及晶片電阻的用量增加。2026 年
AI 需求強勁，稼動率及產品價格已於 2Q26 提升，未來仍有上修空間，預估國巨
2026 年營收成長至 1850.6 億（+39.2%YoY） ，毛利率39.7%(+3.5pts YoY)，營利
率 27.1%(+4.7pts YoY)， 稅後淨利415.5 億元(+75.8%YoY)， 稅後EPS 為 20.23 元。 
展望未來，因產能擴張約需 1-1.5 年，產能缺口難在短期補足，轉單效應將至少延
續至 2Q27，被動元件產業循環仍在持續向上，預期2027 年 MLCC 仍有 30%以上
的漲價空間，其餘被動元件也有望因產能排擠使供給出現缺口帶動下波漲價，故預
估國巨 2027 年營收 2638.2 億（+42.6%YoY） ，毛利率46.1%(+6.4pts YoY)，營利
率 33.4%(+6.3pts YoY)，稅後淨利733.2 億元(+76.5%YoY)，稅後EPS 為 35.4 元。

### Page 1

ASE Technology Holding (3711.TW)
AI semiconductor accelerates multi-year growth and structural Margin 
Upside
CITI'S TAKE 
ASEH delivered another upbeat result, with solid 21% GM and net profit of 
NT$21bn (up 49% QoQ and 180% YoY). It raised its growth outlook (IC ATM 
to be up 35% YoY; LEAP revenue to be over US$3.5bn and doubling into 
2027) and 2026 capex expectations (from US$8.5bn to now US$10.5bn), 
citing stronger-than-anticipated demand for LEAP (advanced packaging 
and testing). With accelerating investment in LEAP , panel-level packaging, 
full-process packaging and future CPO technologies, ASEH believes it is 
well positioned to capture a multi-year AI growth cycle while continuing to 
expand profitability. Maintain Buy and lift TP to NT$750 (25X 2027 EPS).
Capex accelerates to expand advanced packaging leadership — ASEH believes AI 
remains in the early stages of a structural infrastructure buildout, supporting 
sustained demand well beyond current AI accelerator deployments. Demand is also 
broadening beyond AI accelerators into industrial power, connectivity and storage, 
reinforcing confidence that AI infrastructure spending will remain a multi-year 
growth driver. Profitability continues to improve faster than expected as LEAP and 
advanced testing contribute a larger share of revenue. ATM gross margin reached 
27.3% in 2Q26, while management expects margins to improve sequentially and 
potentially exceed the long-standing 30% structural ceiling in 4Q26. Margin 
expansion is being driven by higher utilization, richer product mix, automation and 
operating leverage rather than temporary cost benefits. Pricing also remains 
supportive, with inflationary cost increases largely passed through to customers.
Technology roadmap broadens beyond CoWoS — Other than CoWoS, ASEH is 
investing across multiple next-generation technologies, including panel-based 
packaging, full-process CoWoS packaging and future CPO integration. It expects its 
fully automated panel-level packaging line to begin production in 1Q27, providing a 
scalable platform for larger chiplet architectures. Meanwhile, CPO is expected to 
enter initial production later this year, allowing the industry to validate bandwidth, 
thermal performance and manufacturing yield before broader deployment. 
Management also highlighted future heterogeneous integration combining digital, 
analog and sensor technologies for AI and humanoid applications. ASEH believes its 
neutral ecosystem position enables collaboration across foundries, substrate 
suppliers and customers in various packaging architecture - CoWoS, EMIB, panel-
based or future optical integration, reinforcing its long-term competitive advantage.

Further upward revision, maintain Buy  — Along with its doubling revenue in LEAP ,
we are looking for 40kwpm capacity for ASEH into next year from 20kwpm in
2026. We also lift our earnings projection by 11%/23%/25% for 2026/2027 /2028
thanks to better structural GM expansion and AI-led upside. We maintain our Buy
rating on ASEH with higher TP at NT$750 (25x of our 2027 EPS estimate).
Solid result and constructive outlook

ASEH reports a better than expected 2Q26 result and positive outlook with solid
21% GM and net profit of NT$21bn (up 49% QoQ and 180% YoY). Management
raised both growth (IC ATM to be up 35% YoY; LEAP revenue to be over US$3.5bn
and doubling into 2027) and capex expectations (from US$8.5bn to now
US$10.5bn), citing stronger-than-anticipated demand for LEAP advanced
packaging and testing. Thanks to better product mix and more LEAP revenue
exposure, we see further upward earnings revision ahead.

Key takeaways from earnings call
IC ATM (Assembly, Test & Materials)
•1H26 ATM revenue +35% YoY.
•LEAP revenue ahead of its target of US$3.5bn for 2026; expecting to be
doubling into 2027
•2Q26 ATM revenue reached a record NT$126.1bn, +12% QoQ / +36% YoY.
•Full-year ATM revenue expected to grow ~ 35% YoY.

•3Q26 ATM revenue guidance: +11–13% QoQ.
•LEAP revenue is tracking ahead of the previous US$3.5bn guidance, with
management targeting doubling LEAP revenue in 2027.
•Growth is constrained by capacity expansion and execution, rather than
demand.
•2Q26 ATM GM: 27.3% (+1.3ppt QoQ; +5.4ppt YoY), driven by higher LEAP
mix, better operating leverage, higher utilization.

•3Q26 ATM GM guidance: 28–29%.

•CoWoS continues to support current AI infrastructure while ASEH is also

•Equipment allocation:
o~56% to assembly.
o~40% to testing.
oRemaining to EMS and interconnect materials.
•Around 70% of equipment CapEx is dedicated to leading-edge (LEAP)
technologies, with the remainder supporting mainstream advanced
packaging and testing capacity.
•ASEH is simultaneously executing 13 greenfield projects and 8 brownfield

expansions, which management believes should provide sufficient
capacity through 2028 and potentially into 2029.
EMS

•Management noted the unusually strong sequential growth is primarily

Bull/Bear: ASE Technology Holding (3711.TW)

### Page 10

ASE Technology Holding (3711.TW)
31 July 2026 Citi Research
10
ASE Technology Holding
Company description
Advanced Semiconductor Engineering (ASE), incorporated in 1984 and listed
at the TWSE in 1989, is one of the world's leading companies in the
semiconductor packaging and testing sector. In June 2016, ASE entered into a
Joint Share Exchange Agreement with SPIL under which ASE Industrial was
eventually to be formed as a holding company with 100% equity interests in

both ASE and SPIL. ASE Industrial was listed on Taiwan's main board on April
30, 2018, the same day on which ASE and SPIL were delisted.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on ASE Holding. The combined ASE-SPIL entity is the
highest-quality OSAT worldwide, in our view, with its market share
leadership, superior operations, economies of scale, and customer/product
diversification. Conditions imposed by the Chinese government on the ASE-
SPIL merger, which required them to operate independently, were lifted in

2020. Consequently, we believe ASE Holding will benefit from further revenue
and operating synergies as well as the trend for advanced packaging.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$750 is based on 25x 2027 EPS average. Our PER is
higher than its five-year range as we expect ASEH to see accelerating
earnings growth in the coming years with GM at upward expansion trend,
mainly driven by its promising outlook in LEAP business and strong expansion
for long-term AI demand. At our target price, the shares would trade at a

2026/27E P/B of 8x/7x.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent ASE shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) poor efficiency and less synergy created from merger with
SPIL; 2) failure to gain market share in advanced packaging; 3) industry
oversupply due to aggressive capacity expansion; 4) severe pricing erosion
due to competition; and 5) a longer-than-expected semiconductor inventory
correction.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

### Page 1

M
Update
Realtek Semiconductor  | Asia Pacific
Better than feared outlook
The stock appears to be a safe harbor vs. other high-flyers (e.g.,
Memory) following the trough into 2Q earnings .
2Q review: 2Q revenue was NT$37.6bn, +3% Q/Q and +18% Y/Y, 3% above MSe. 2Q
gross profit was NT$17.7bn. GM was 47.0%, -2.7ppt Q/Q and -3.2ppt Y/Y, 2.7ppt
below MSe. EPS was NT$7.42, -12% Q/Q and -3% Y/Y, 12% below MSe. Margin
compression was driven by elevated memory pricing and constrained OSAT capacity.

2H26 outlook: PC segment faces demand pressure in 2H26 due to elevated memory
costs and CPU supply tightness, but revenue growth is expected, helped by spec-up
demand. For the consumer segment, TV shipments have begun to soften after FIFA;
home appliance demand continues to grow steadily and global game console
shipments are expected to fall this year. The networking segment demonstrated
demand stability, supported by enterprise infra refresh cycles and the transition

toward higher-value WiFi7 and multi-gig Ethernet solutions. The auto segment is
expected to be seasonally strong in 2H, especially helped by growth from the
European market.
Other highlights: Increased product pricing with certain customers is to take effect
in 3Q, helping GM recover. Realtek is gaining share in the managed switch market,
having increased 2-3ppt vs the previous reporting period. WiFi7 penetration in
notebooks is estimated to reach ~30% this year, while WiFi8 deployment is seen

starting from 1Q28.
Remain EW: The company is indeed showing better than feared outlook due to
FOMP (link). We believe the stock is fairly valued relative to our implied target of
19x 2026E P/E. Supply constraints and weak end-market demand continues, but we
expect the company to manage some revenue growth in 3Q and a continued GM
rebound in 2H, helped by spec-up demand and price increases.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst

Estimate revisions and quarterly financials
We cut our EPS estimates by 12% each for 2026 and 2027 EPS, and raise 2028 by 1%:
For 2026-27, we factor in actual 2Q26 results, near-term end demand weakness and
supply constraints. We expect some demand recovery and supply relief into 2028.

Valuation methodology
Our PT is unchanged at NT$717: We factor in changes to our earnings estimates while
keeping other parameters in our residual income model unchanged. We maintain our
assumptions for cost of equity of 9.6%, intermediate growth rate of 7.3%, payout ratio of
79%, and terminal growth of 4.5%. Our bull and bear case scenario values are both
unchanged at NT$1117 and NT$517, respectively.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

### Page 1

ab 30 July 2026
Global Research
Realtek Semiconductor Corp
Better-than-feared results and outlook
H2 sales holds up on price adjustment and stable demand
Realtek's Q2 sales were up 3% QoQ at NT$37.6bn, lower than consensus and UBSe of
NT$38.0bn/39.7bn. Looking ahead, sales in H2 should stay rather resilient even with
consumer demand weakness across certain segments, considering 1) June product price
hike to be factored into H2 sales, especially for products impacted by memory price

increase. 2) Stable demand from networking, automotive, and home appliances
categories. 3) Despite soft consumer end demand, Realtek's customers are still pre-
building inventory given tightening industry supply especially at backend and memory
YTD. We estimate Q3 sales to trend slightly higher and grow by 9% QoQ, better than
consensus of -3% QoQ, mainly thanks to price hike. Q4 sales could decline seasonally by
7% QoQ. Realtek’s inventory level appears healthy, with DOI at 107 days in Q2 vs 105

days in Q1.
GM weaker in Q2 but should improve in H2
Q2 GM was 47.0%, down 2.7ppts from Q1, as the company had to absorb component
cost increases (e.g. DRAM/OSAT tightness) during the quarter. Realtek started to
negotiate pricing adjustment with customers in June on a case-by-case basis,
considering further cost inflation. We believe Realtek's GM impact can be mitigated and
GM should pick up gradually in H2. We are estimating Q3/Q4's GM to be
47.9%/48.6%.

Earnings estimate revisions
We raise 2026E/27E sales by 3%/13%, to 25%/18% YoY growth, reflecting upside
from price adjustment and more steady demand environment. We estimate opex to be
kept at around 37% for Q3 and Q426, as the company continues to conduct cost
control measures to optimise profitability. These changes result in EPS increases of
2%/18%/12% in 2026E/27E/28E.
Valuation: maintain Neutral; raise our price target  from NT$525 to NT$690

We maintain our Neutral rating given the uncertainty about consumer demand due to
memory cost induced pricing pressure. Our new price target is based on 17x 2027E PE vs
16x 2026E PE previously, justified by our higher LT earnings CAGR estimate of 16%,
from 14% prior.

### Page 2

Realtek Semiconductor Corp UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Can Realtek sustain its H126 sales growth of 10.5% YoY in H226?
Realtek’s sales should hold up better than feared into H2 and grow 41% YoY. We expect key drivers
to be price adjustments as Realtek passes through higher cost alongside mix upgrade. In terms of
demand, although consumer and PC restocking are slowing down, demand from networking, auto,

and IoT remains steady.
Q: Can Realtek maintain its GM in H226 and in 2027 at the current level given cost inflation?
We forecast gross margin to have a sequential recovery in H226, at 48.3%, from Q2’s 47.0%,
assuming Realtek can pass through the rising supply chain cost including foundry and packaging with
its ongoing pricing negotiations with end customers. However, if demand further weakens into late
2026, there could be some headwinds in the short term as Realtek starts to see challenges to fully

passing through the higher production cost, and product mix may also turn more unfavorable.
We believe sales in H2 should stay rather resilient even with consumer demand weakness across
certain segments, considering 1) June product price hike to be factored into H2 sales, especially for
products impacted by memory price increase. 2) Stable demand from networking, automotive, and
home appliances categories. 3) Despite soft consumer end demand, Realtek's customers are still pre-

building inventory given tightening industry supply especially at backend and memory YTD.
Realtek indicated it has negotiated pricing adjustments on a case-by-case basis with its customers in
June 2026, to offset higher cost pressure from supply chain tightness and rising component costs.
This should bode well for sales and margins in H226.
Realtek is trading at 17x 2027E PE, higher than its historical average of 15x forward PE. We believe
the market could be optimistic about price hikes and better sales momentum, alongside Realtek's

ability to hold up margin.

Founded in 1987, Realtek Semiconductor (Realtek) is one of the world's leading integrated circuit (IC)

design houses. It has technological expertise in digital and mixed-signal IC design, and its top five
product lines in terms of sales are Wi-Fi, Ethernet switches, TV SoCs, Ethernet and audio codec.

Company Description
Founded in 1987, Realtek Semiconductor (Realtek) is one of the world's leading integrated
circuit (IC) design houses. It has technological expertise in digital and mixed-signal IC design,
and its top five product lines in terms of sales are Wi-Fi, Ethernet switches, TV SoCs, Ethernet

and audio codec. Realtek's main competitors in the networking space are Broadcom, Marvell,
MediaTek and Qualcomm.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We derive our price target using PE valuation, relative to the historical trading range.
Key risks include: 1) higher/lower pricing pressure than expected; 2) a faster/slower
networking upgrade; 3) faster-than-expected/slower-than-expected penetration of
automotive Ethernet; and 4) a shorter-than-expected or longer-than-expected qualification

period for Realtek to make progress in new verticals.

the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

## Document 105: 260731_daiwa_mtk UG

Source PDF: all report pdf/260731_daiwa_mtk UG.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-07-31
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: MediaTek (MTK) released upbeat 2Q26 results but downbeat 
3Q26 guidance. Though the cash-cow mobile business looks to be 
weakening on commodity cost inflation, its new AI business appears to be 
running ahead of expectation. We raise our AI accelerator assumption and 
expect MTK’s AI ASIC revenue contribution to overtake Mobile in mid-2027, 
a year earlier than our previous forecast. Aided by the recent reset of equity 
market’s hefty expectation unlocking value for reinvestment, in our view, we 
upgrade our rating 2 notches to Buy (1) with a refreshed TP of TWD4,088.

What’s the impact: Results upbeat but guidance downbeat. MTK 
reported 2Q26 net profit of TWD24bn, 5%/8% above our/consensus 
estimates, thanks to lower income tax and higher top line, which beat 
guidance (TWD140-149bn) and market expectations on better share gain 
in its Smart-edge business, partially offset by lower margins (table on p.2 
for more details). Guided 3Q26 top line missed our previous forecast due to 
weaker-than-expected Mobile business, where management failed to 
realise its previous expectation calling 2Q26 as the trough, though it 
maintains its forecast for this year’s global smartphone shipment to drop 
c.15% YoY. That said, it sees its AI ASIC business as progressing even 
stronger for “substantial” growth into 2027.

Weaker Mobile more than offset by stronger AI. Following its upward 
revision in the previous quarter, MTK at today’s call further lifted its AI 
accelerator (ex-HBM) serviceable addressable market (SAM) to USD80bn 
for 2027 (previous: USD70-80bn), targeting a market share of 15-20% 
(previous: 10-15%), which translates into USD12-16bn potential revenue 
for the company. This suggests its first AI ASIC for a hyperscale customer 
is on track for mass production (MP) in 4Q26, while the second chip for the 
same customer is on track for MP by early 2028, with higher dollar value on 
stronger performance/watt and lower total cost of ownership (TCO). Net of 
the AI strength and mobile weakness, we raise our 2027-28E EPS by 23-
30%, where we now forecast MTK’s AI ASIC revenue to reach USD2.1bn, 
USD15bn, and USD22bn for 2026, 2027 and 2028, respectively.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we lift our 12M TP for 
MTK to TWD4,088 (previous: TWD2,454), now based on our 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 36x (previous: 27x), justified by our 2025-28E EPS 
CAGR of 36% on 1x PEG. We upgrade our rating 2 notches to Buy (1) with 
key downside risk of any AI accelerator ramps below our expectation.

How we differ: While our 2026-27E EPS are 7-10% above the consensus 
forecasts, we see the street as too aggressive on MTK’s 2028 AI business. 
e

Company profile 
Ranked No.5 in the world by 2023 fabless semiconductor revenue, MediaTek (MTK) offers diverse 
products across optical storage, digital home, smartphone, PC, ASIC, automotive and other IoT 
applications. MTK has successfully captured the industry demand cycles from optical disk drives to 
feature phones to smartphones, and now positions itself well as a platform provider to capitalise on the 
next demand cycle of the BigData/AI to help facilitate digital transformation in human lives.

### Page 1

M
Update
MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
Raising  2027 ASIC revenue and
more confident about 2028;
reiterate  OW
Increase in 2027 TPU revenue forecast should still be an upside surprise: The
company now expects it can gain 15%-20% revenue share in 2027 ASIC TAM of US
$80bn (excluding HBM cost). That implies US$12bn-US$16bn revenue, above its
prior outlook for US$7bn-US$12bn. We project 2027 TPU revenue at US$15.7bn.
Because TPU is the major ASIC project in global TAM, MediaTek’s implied TPU

market share could be 20% in 2027, similar to our preview.
Confident in 2nm TPU adopting EMIB-T packaging in 2028: The company is
confident that EMIB-T substrate yield can improve. We asked whether this project
still needs TSMC’s CoWoS capacity as a back-up, and management replied that
“switch to a second source would take time,” which to us means no need for now.
MediaTek also believes its ASIC revenue market share would further increase in
2028—very bullish, in our view.

3Q26 revenue guided to be flat to up 5% Q/Q, better than our down 5% Q/Q: We
cite a sustainable high-end smartphone segment and MediaTek’s chip price hike.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside

n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.
n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 117: 260802_ms_hyperscaler-results

Source PDF: all report pdf/260802_ms_hyperscaler-results.pdf

Date: 2026-08-02
Pages: 11

### Page 1

M
Idea
Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
Read-across from US
Hyperscalers' 2Q26 Results
We view US hyperscalers' comments and capex guidance
positively for supply chain companies in Greater China. There are
numerous beneficiaries in our hardware coverage; we highlight
Wiwynn, Wistron,  Gold Circuit, Unimicron, NYPCB and Lenovo.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Howard Kao
Equity Analyst
Howard.Kao@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2989

Irene Yen
Research Associate
Irene.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2869
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Andy Meng, CFA
Equity Analyst
Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7689
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
US hyperscalers' comments positive for the supply chain: US hyperscaler
commentary was  uniformly positive, with demand still outstripping supply. Higher

capex guidance was expected given rising component prices, but it shows
hyperscalers are raising spending to absorb those costs rather than cutting unit
volumes. That should ease investor concerns that higher component prices would
reduce overall server unit procurement. Although most hyperscalers did not provide
CY27-28 capex guidance, their comments hinted that demand will remain robust
into 2027, with Amazon calling CY28 demand "striking."
Stay OW on supply chain: We continue to like Wiwynn; it is a key general server

supplier to three hyperscalers (Microsoft, Meta and Amazon) and could potentially
become an assembly partner to a new hyperscale customer. Wiwynn is also a key
supplier of Trainium server racks and AMD Helios racks for Meta. We like Wistron
as it is a key L6 partner to Nvidia and AMD, with more L10 and L11 exposure for AI
servers. We like GCE as a beneficiary of general server and ASIC PCB for US
hyperscalers. We like Unimicron and NYPCB, as they are the key ABF substrate

suppliers for compute (CPU/GPU/ASIC) and networking chips in Greater China. We
like Lenovo as it is a key general server supplier to Microsoft and Oracle.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
MSFT maintained guidance for capex to be US$190B (US$175B after
incorporating for the lease changes).
MSFT F1Q27/C3Q26 capex guided >US$50B, a meaningful step-up from US$41B
in F4Q26/C2Q26.
Meta CY26 capex narrowed from US$125-145B to US$130-145B; still no guidance
on CY27 capex.
GOOG raised CY26 capex guidance by US$15B to US$195-205B, and again said

CY27 would increase "significantly" y/y.
AWS raised CY26 capex by US$20B to US$220B on higher memory costs; most
compute capacity in 2027 is already reserved, and 2028 demand is said to be
"striking."
August 2, 2026 11:46 PM GMT

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 119: 260803_gs_kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/260803_gs_kinsus.pdf
Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

We hosted Kinsus’ 2Q26 post-result follow-up call today (Aug 
3rd). Key takeaways include: 
(1) The company didn’t rule out the possibility of a potential supply 
shortage for ABF substrate in 2027/28 (previously mentioned that 
high layer count ABF substrate market to be in SD balance while 
expecting low layer count ABF substrate market to be in oversupply) 
as the company is receiving strong forecasts for next-gen AI related 
products with packaging size and layer counts that continue to go 
up. 
(2) In terms of pricing, Kinsus expect ABF and BT substrate pricing to 
have gone up by ~8% QoQ on average in 3Q/4Q26 with ABF 
substrate pricing to increase faster than BT substrate (reversing its 
previous expectation that BT substrate pricing to increase faster 
than ABF substrate in 2H26). The company expects revenue to see 
+10% QoQ in both 3Q/4Q26 (vs. 13% QoQ in 2Q26) and expects 
GM to reach 27-28/29% in 3Q/4Q26 (vs. 26% in 2Q26). 
(3) Without considering the potential pricing hike on ABF substrate 
going into 2027/28, Kinsus expects revenue in 2027/28 to increase 
at least 30% YoY per year while expecting GM to improve by 5ppt 
each year in 2027/28 as the company will continue to focus on 
product mix improvement and reaching higher UTR.  
(4) Kinsus announced a project CAPEX of NT$19.6bn, which will be 
split into 3 years and result in upward revision in 2026/27 CAPEX of 
~NT$14bn/~NT$16bn (75%+/60%+ upward revision from 
NT$8bn/NT$10bn). The project CAPEX is driven entirely by new 
facility construction while equipment is sponsored by key customers 
(3-4 customers or more). Moreover, Kinsus is considering to expand 
K7 and K8 Plant in Taiwan as customers seek to secure capacity 
beyond 2030, while capacity and investment scale have yet to be 
finalized.

### Page 3

(5) For AI-related products, Kinsus is targeting 100% market share for Vera CPU
substrate (previously targeting 50-100% market share for AI CPU substrate), expanding
from the current 50% share for Grace CPU substrate. The company continues to expect
its AI GPU market share to go up to 20% in coming quarters (vs. 10% now). Moreover,
the company also targets to gain market share in AMD/META/AWS’s AI accelerator
substrates in 2H26 and expects to further expand its share. The company expects AI

contribution to reach ~10% of total revenue in 2026, and expand to 15-20% in 2027.
(6) In terms of BT substrate demand, the company mentioned optical modules as being
one of the key drivers for the first time, given the PCB for optical modules applies a
similar production process to BT substrate (mSAP solutions), and expecting this segment
to reach 5% of total revenue in 2027 from <1% in 2026. Another driver for the BT
substrate business included demand from memory that will continue to grow in coming

years.
(7) In terms of BT substrate supply, the company hinted that it may potentially expand
capacity in China in 2027 to capture spillover orders from other ABF substrate suppliers,
as peers continue to convert BT substrate capacity to ABF substrate production (see
here).
(8) For future product opportunities, Kinsus is currently developing both glass/ceramic
core substrate, it is still at a very early stage of R&D and expects no/very limited revenue

contribution from this product prior to 2029.
(9) Kinsus reiteratedcits goal of reaching 80mn units of ABF capacity per month by the
end of 2029 (doubling capacity vs. now), with total facility construction spending
expected to be NT$45bn while the company mentioned that key customers will spend
NT$60-80bn for the equipment (with NT$60bn already secured) throughout 2026-29.
These customers will own 30mn units of the 2029 end capacity (~38% of total capacity)

due to its direct equipment investment.
Investment view
We are positive on Kinsus’ 2Q result (the solid jump on GM in 2Q -see here), and expect
the company to further deliver a strong revenue/GM expansion in 2H26 thanks to the
much better than expected market share on the high volume NVDA Vera CPU substrate
(the company expects 100% market share for Vera CPU substrate, while the company
only expected 50%+ three months ago – see here), which should contribute ~10%/15%

of the company’s ABF revenue or 15/20%+ of the ABF profit contribution in 2026/27E
with a much higher than company’s average GM/OPM.
Moreover, Kinsus’ tone on ABF substrates S/D outlook turns much more favorable (and
echos our view on more tightness on ABF substrates in coming years -see here), while
the company sees more and more high-layer/large size projects have been introduced
by customers who are asking for more capacity from Kinsus to support the demand,

despite the company having no numerical data to support this assumption, and still
input any like for like base pricing hike assumption in their +30% YoY revenue guidance
for 2027/28 or +5ppt GM YoY growth in 2027 &2028.
Going into LT, the company’s (1) new CAPEX raise (see here), (2) its new comment on
potential new K7/8 plant for 2030 and beyond new capacity expansion, and (3)
customers’ direct investment in ABF substrate capacity for more than NT$80bn in the

3 August 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Kinsus (3189.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

next 3 years, should imply a long term demand sustainability and could improve investor 
confidence levels on long term AI demand sustainability. 
Overall, we maintain our Buy rating on Kinsus, and we expect the better market share in 
AI server CPU and its better position in the high-end ABF substrate industry into long 
term. We revise up our Kinsus TP to NT$1,125 (from NT$1,120). 
Earnings revisions 
We revise up our 2027/28/29E earnings estimate by 5/10/10% to factor in the solid 
GM/OPM expansion from a more favorable pricing outlook given the potential supply 
shortage environment (revise up 2027/28/29 GM by 0.9/1.7/1.2ppt and OPM by 
0.9/1.7/1.2ppt). 
We revise down our 2026E earnings estimate by 5% after reflecting a more conservative 
margin assumption on its IC substrate business in 2H26E, though we expect solid 
GM/OPM expansion in coming quarters.

Valuation 
We maintain our Buy rating with a new 12m TP of NT$1,125 (from NT$1,120), based on 
16.5x 2028E P/E, which is +1.2x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average P/E 
(14.2x), and factor in our latest earnings revisions.

### Page 5

Investment Thesis - Kinsus
Kinsus is one of the key IC substrates suppliers globally with ~10% ABF/BT substrates
market share in 2024, with ~18% of total 2025 revenue contributed by its contact lens
subsidiary, Pegavision (6941.TW, Not Covered). With our view that the ABF substrate
shortage started from 2Q26 (ABF substrate return to supply demand balance from late
2025), we believe Kinsus’ performance will continue to improve compared with high-end

ABF peers considering the company’s increasing market share in the high-end ABF
substrate market; however, ~36% of the company’s revenue was contributed by BT
substrates in 2025 and 30% of the company’s ABF revenue is from non-LTA customers,
which we believe will continue to enjoy a better pricing outlook. As a result, we believe
Kinsus’ earnings outlook should continue to be strong (~106% 2026-29E CAGR), mainly
driven by better BT substrate and non-LTA ABF substrate pricing outlook, despite the

unfavorable demand outlook before 2H26. We believe Kinsus is an experienced
substrate supplier with a clear capacity expansion plan that can also upgrade its product
portfolio in the long term. We believe we are at the beginning of an industry upcycle,
making us positive on Kinsus’ stock performance over the next 12 months.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Kinsus
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,125 for Kinsus is based on

16.5x 2028E P/E, which is +1.2x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average P/E
(14.2x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, (3)
Slower/Less-than-expected AI server orders from 2H26.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total

## Document 124: 260804_daiwa_Fositek

Source PDF: all report pdf/260804_daiwa_Fositek.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-04
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We attended Fositek’s investor meeting following its 2Q26 
results announcement on 4 August. We believe the company will benefit 
from the AI server trend with rising liquid cooling penetration rate in the 
following years, with rising server revenue over 2026-28E. Given the 
company’s share-gain potential, we reaffirm our Buy (1) rating. See our 
memo, 7 July 2026.

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results missed, but historical-high margins 
set. Fositek’s 2Q26 revenue of TWD3,740m (-10.6% QoQ, +42.7% YoY) 
missed our and Bloomberg consensus estimates by 12.1% and 12.8%, 
respectively, due to the product transition in the foldable phone and server 
segments. Its 2Q26 gross margin came in at 34.0% (vs. 32.3% in 1Q26 
and 24.1% in 2Q25), a record-high level above our (33.0%) and consensus 
(32.9%) estimates, mainly on manufacturing process improvements and 
better-than-expected product mix (server revenue accounted for 55.7% in 
1H26, up from 36.3% in 2025). Its operating margin of 27.9% (vs. 26.8% in 
1Q26 and 19.0% in 2Q25), also a record-high level, beat our (27.2%) and 
consensus (26.9%) estimates, on better opex control. Thus, its operating 
profit of TWD1,043m (-6.9% QoQ, +110.0% YoY) missed our and 
consensus estimates by 9.9% and 9.5%, respectively. Overall, its net profit 
of TWD849m (-7.4% QoQ, +157.0% YoY) missed our and consensus 
estimates by 16.0% and 12.4%, respectively. Its 2Q26 EPS finished at 
TWD12.39. Its 2Q26 product mix was: server (54.9%, +259.9% YoY), 
foldable phone (38.3%, -20.5% YoY), NB (6.0%, -0.8% YoY) and others 
(0.7%, +49.1% YoY).

Brighter 2H26 vs. 1H26. We expect a strong 2H26 revenue vs. 1H26 and 
gross margin expansion on the ramping up of GPU and ASIC projects in 
2H26. Due to the rise in liquid cooling penetration rate, product application 
and QD shipments per rack, we expect its revenue to grow 40.9% YoY in 
2026E and 41.7% YoY in 2027E. We lower our 2026-28E EPS by 5-11% 
due to our slightly conservative revenue and higher tax rate estimates.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD2,615 (from TWD2,600), now based on a target PER of 32x (from 
33x vs. its past-3-year range of 9-43x [average of 24x]) on decelerating 
earnings momentum, translating to a 0.8x PEG over 2026-28E on our 1-
year forward EPS. Key downside risks: lower-than-expected market share 
for key clients, slower-than-expected penetration rate of foldable phones 
and geopolitical risks.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 5-8% below the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more conservative on revenue.

4 August 2026

Investor meeting: positive tone over 2026-27 outlook

➢ 2Q26 results missed, but historical-high gross margins 
➢ Stronger revenue outlook in 2H26 vs. 1H26, driven by server segment  
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; raising 12M TP to TWD2,615

## Document 125: 260804_daiwa_Nanya Technology

### Page 1

Summary: Nanya announced its July revenue after market hours on 4 August. Its revenue 
of TWD 44bn increased significantly MoM (49%) and YoY (720%). Given limited shipment 
growth due to supply constraints, we assume higher DRAM price in July and 3Q26 on solid 
demand. Assuming August-September revenue rises 6-8% MoM as it did in 2Q26, we 
expect Nanya’s 3Q26 revenue to reach TWD142bn, exceeding our and market estimates 
by 47% and 37%, respectively. We assume DDR4 DRAM ASP to increase by >30% QoQ 
in 3Q26, likely to drive another earnings surprise following 2Q26.

Recommendation 
We have a Buy (1) call on Nanya with a 12M TP of TWD650. We expect the robust July 
revenue and positive earnings outlook for 3Q26 to drive positive investor sentiment. Key 
risk: weaker-than-expected DRAM demand due to delays in data centre build-outs.

## Document 127: 260804_gs_airtac

Source PDF: all report pdf/260804_gs_airtac.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-04

### Page 2

For the month of August 2026, we estimate AirTAC’s monthly revenue will slightly 
decline to Rmb828mn (-1% MoM, +35% YoY)/NT$3,934mn (-1% MoM, +52% YoY) 
We expect August revenue will go down by 1% MoM to Rmb828mn/NT$3,934mn, 
mainly due to 6% less working days in Mainland China, while the growth remains strong 
from key customers (including battery, electronics and machine tools) and overall 
pneumatic UTR remains high at 100% despite in the low seasons. The company’s 
backlog continues to increase as of the end of July, and we expect shipment to be solid 
in August in order to fulfill these undelivered orders, thus we believe the overall daily 
revenue in August should be higher than July (Rmb31.0mn vs. Rmb29.8mn).

Investment view: Shipment and order continue to be better than expected since 2026 
start. Maintain positive view on AirTAC 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC, as we remain positive on the company’s previous 
comment on the (1) solid 2026 revenue outlook, which is expected to see 20%+ YoY 
growth (see here), and (2) a prolonged upcycle that could last longer at 2-3 years this 
time with robust performance expected to sustain across multiple end markets, 
including battery/electronics/automotive/machine tools. Furthermore, the company will

### Page 3

continue to enhance internal operational efficiency to mitigate the ongoing risk of higher 
raw material costs amid geopolitical uncertainties. Moreover, the company remains 
committed to maintain a stable pricing strategy in the China pneumatic market, which 
we believe should support further market share gains. We continue to see backlog 
accumulation and expect the company to maintain a high UTR to effectively digest the 
growing order backlog. 
Also, we believe AirTAC’s strong B/S and healthy cash flow position will help the 
company to overcome potential industry demand risks, and further supporting market 
share gains during industry downturns (AirTAC’s share gains have accelerated during 
industry downcycles historically, due to its solid B/S and cash flow management). We 
expect the company’s ROE to continue to rise in the coming years (ROE should improve 
to 26.2% in 2028E per GSe vs. 16%-17% in 2022-25) supporting a high dividend payout 
ratio (50%-60%+ per year per GSe) in coming years. 
Maintain Buy on AirTAC with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,925. 
Investment Thesis - AirTAC 
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share 
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality 
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast 
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner 
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC 
continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing 
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and 
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure 
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring 
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to 
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring. 
Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its 
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the 
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to 
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E 
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC. 
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,925 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on 
our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle). 
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected 
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in 
mainland China.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate

value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.

CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Source PDF: all report pdf/3023信邦 國票.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-03

請參閱末頁之聲明

2026.08.03 
洪有辰 C00270@ibfs.com.tw 
3023.TT 信邦 
 用 MAGIC 產業創造更多 MAGIC！

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
信邦成立於 1989 年，總部設立於新北市，為精密連接線束與相關組件大廠，透過醫
療器材、車用航太、綠能環境、工業應用與通訊等五大產業的布局，為全球客戶提供
高附加價值的連接線組服務。近期在機器人、半導體設備與低軌衛星 等也有所著墨。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 365 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作 ，362 
【大盤指數/股價】43120 / 296

➢ 2Q26 工業 、 半導體與機器人等領域小量出貨 ， 產品組合持續優化 
信邦 2Q26 營收達 90.19 億元，QoQ+8.8%、YoY+16.6%，主因綠能、
工業需求回升 ， 且半導體 、 機器人與無人機開始小量出貨 ； 毛利率24.5%
持平上季，雖受低毛利的伺服器機櫃及汽車產品出貨增加衝擊，但高毛
利的客製化工業應用占比提升，抵銷了部分跌幅；營業利益率 10.4%， 
-1.1pts. QoQ，-0.9pts. YoY，主要受新研發、調薪與運費導致費用率上
升影響；業外因匯兌與金融評價利益增加，助攻獲利，2Q26 稅後 EPS 
3.76 元，YoY+19%；累計 1H26 營收 173.11 億元，YoY+8.9%，稅後
EPS 7.37 元，YoY+5%。 
➢ MAGIC 五大應用中，工業占比最大、汽車成長性最高 
信邦在工業應用因人形機器人、智慧交通、半導體設備與AI 基礎建設等
實體 AI 四大核心領域開始迎來收割期，2026 年與 2027 年占比將擴大
至 31.7%與 34%。其中，人形機器人已打入5 家美企，單台線束價值逾
1,500 美元，並結盟正淩與宇隆，預計2026 年及 2027 年出貨 1 萬與 2
萬套 ， 台中新廠2H27 量產後更將加速成長 。 汽車領域則具最高成長性 ，
預估 2026 年與 2027 年增幅達 41.4%與 23.3%。公司策略調整有成：
中國轉向服務 比亞迪(002594.CH)等在地一線新能源車廠；日本市場靠
液冷快充直銷日系大廠；美歐市場則受惠電動車發展，除完成 NACS 充
電線束量產準備，亦積極參展推廣歐規輕量化充電產品。 
➢ 2026~2027 年多項題材陸續發酵 ，調升至買進建議， 目標價365 元 
投研部預估信邦 2026 年/2027 全年營收分別為 361.51 億元/415.97 億
元，YoY+16.5% /15.1%；公司在人形機器人、AI 伺服器機櫃專用的大
電流線束 、 半導體設備與低軌衛星通訊用線束等高毛利產品將陸續出貨 ，
預估毛利率將上升至 24.6%/24.8%；隨著高毛利產品量產後，初期較高
的研發費用將逐漸被抵銷，預估稅後 EPS 分別為 15.19 元/18.16 元。在
評價方面 ， 目前PER 為 19.5X / 16.3X， 過去兩年PER 區間在 14X~26X，
考量公司在台灣與海外所布局的 MAGIC 五大高階應用領域深具潛力，
其中長期 TAM(總潛在市場)仍將處於高速成長階段，信邦將逐步在多地
擴大滲透率，評價有提升的空間，因此調升至買進評等，目標價 365 元
(2027F PER 20X)。

### Page 2

- 2 -

個股報告

公司簡介 
信邦成立於 1989 年 ， 總部設立於台灣新北市汐止區 ， 為高階連接線束與精密連接器組
件的領導大廠 ， 目前已由早期的低毛利消費性電子代工廠 ， 成功轉型為高客製化與高附
加價值的系統整合方案提供商 。 公司為避免單一產業的景氣循環波動 ， 以共同設計與製
造的方式跨足統稱為 「MAGIC」 的五大高階應用領域：1) M (Medical，醫療器材)：高
精密醫療級連接線束 ，應用於斷層掃描 、 超音波與心導管設備等高門檻領域 。醫療線束
對訊號傳輸的零失真與抗干擾(EMI)要求極高，且必需通過生物相容性檢驗，信邦旗下
產品具備耐高溫高壓滅菌與耐重複彎折數十萬次等特性，供應國際一線醫療設備大廠
如 GE 與 Philips 等；2) A (Automotive，車用與航太)：電動車用高壓動力線束、感測
系統、以及全球首張通過 UL 安全認證的 DC 直流快充液冷系統(HPC)。車用線束需長
期暴露於高低溫差 、 劇烈震動與高電壓的環境中 ， 信邦的液冷快充槍透過在線纜內注入
冷卻液，成功在縮小線徑、減輕重量的同時，實現超高功率充電而不發燙，切入歐美地
區的充電基礎設施市場；3) G (Green，綠能環境)：風力發電大型控制櫃線束、太陽能
逆變器連接線等綠色能源基礎設施 。 綠能產品面臨極端氣候的考驗 ， 如離岸風電的鹽霧
腐蝕及陸域風電的極地低溫 ， 信邦除了供應相關線材 ， 也具備大型控制櫃的組裝與測試
能力，為全球風電巨頭如 Vestas (VWS.CO)的長期戰略夥伴，儘管綠能產業近年受到
美國政策波動影響，但在「2050 淨零碳排」的框架下，全球對綠能基礎建設線材依賴
度依然不減；4) I (Industrial，工業應用)、工業自動化機械手臂關節線、半導體整機系
統模組組裝 、 人形機器人全身關鍵線束 、 半導體高階微影與蝕刻設備無塵室線束 。該領
域為信邦近年成長動能最強勁的板塊 ， 在人形機器人應用場景中 ， 機器人全身擁有數十
個靈活的動力關節 ， 需承受極高頻率的扭轉與拉伸， 且空間極度壓縮 ，信邦目前已成功
打入國際人形機器人新創企業供應鏈， 並取得唯一供應商資格 。而在半導體領域 ，公司
也已從零件供應商升級為設備整機組裝企業，已成為國際科技大廠的合作夥伴； 5) C 
(Communication，通訊週邊)：物聯網裝置、雲端資料中心高速微細同軸線、AI 伺服
器液冷散熱模組線組、低軌衛星的地面接收站線材、5G 微基站戶外防水連接線。隨著
AI 伺服器功耗暴增，傳統風扇散熱難以負荷，信邦開發了用於測試與連接散熱器性能
的液冷線組，其 4U 浸沒式微型液冷運算模組與 Dow 進行深度戰略合作。公司同時也
透過旗下子公司睿信電子在太空經濟中也參與了低軌衛星地面接收裝置的高頻連接線
材研發，為 SpaceX(星鏈計畫 Starlink)的供應商。

2Q26 工業、半導體與機器人等領域小量出貨，產品組合持續優化 
信邦 2Q26 營收 90.19 億元，QoQ+8.8%，YoY+16.6%，主因為綠能需求增溫、高附
加價值的工業應用需求持續增溫 ， 再加上半導體設備 、 人形機器人與無人機等領域開始
小量出貨所致。觀察營收細項，綠色能源佔 18.6%、工業應用佔 35.6%、醫療照護佔
9.7%、汽車佔 16.6%、通訊佔 19.5%；毛利率 24.5%，持平於上季，YoY-0.2pts.，受
伺服器機櫃組裝業務比重提升影響，而該業務材料成本占比較高、附加價值相對較低，
加上汽車事業低毛利產品出貨比重增加 ， 對整體毛利率形成壓力 ， 然而工業應用產品線
具備高度客製化及較高的認證門檻 ， 擁有較佳的獲利能力 ， 隨著其營收占比提升 ，部分
抵銷了前述不利因素，使 2Q26 毛利率仍得以維持與 1Q26 相當的水準；營業利益率
10.4%，-1.1pts. QoQ，-0.9pts. YoY，衰退的原因來自公司在機櫃組裝投入較多新產
品研發費用 ， 同時還調整薪資 、 拓展市場以及支付相關運費 ， 導致費用率YoY+0.7pts.；
在營業外損益部分 ， 因匯兌損失減少及金融資產評價利益增加 ，2Q26 合併營業外利益
較去年同期增加 4,743 萬元，成為獲利成長的來源；整體而言，公司在 2Q26 的產品
組合持續優化，稅後 EPS 3.76 元，QoQ+4.4%，YoY+19%。累計 1H26 營收 173.11
億元，YoY+8.9%；累計 1H26 稅後 EPS 7.37 元，YoY+5%。 
圖 2：信邦營收組合

資料來源：信邦年報、國票投顧整理 
MAGIC 五大應用中，工業占比最大、汽車成長性最高 
信邦 MAGIC 五大應用中，2025 年在醫療照護的營收佔比為 8.8%、汽車占比 16.1%、
綠色能源占比 20.3%、工業應用 30.6%、通訊 24.2%。 
展望未來， 投研部預估信邦在 2026 年與 2027 年占比仍然最大 ， 分別達31.7%與 34%，
主因為公司在實體 AI 的四大核心應用即將四件齊發，包含 1)人形機器人：因配線涵蓋
從頭到腳的範圍，單台機器人線束價值超過 1,500 美元，高於消費性電子與部分車款
電動車的單量線束價值， 且毛利率預估介於30%~35%， 為公司下一波的成長動能， 目
前已打入 5 家美系機器人公司，其中甚至成為 Figure 關鍵線束的唯一供應商。為了將
機器人線束與內部機電整合 ， 公司除了結盟轉投資的連接器公司正淩(8147)之外 ， 也與
高階精密金屬加工廠宇隆(2233)簽署合作備忘錄，由信邦負責高靈敏度線束與連接器、
電控模組以及 PCB 板，而宇隆負責 RV 減速機、行星減速機與諧波減速機。目前公司
已邁入少量至中量試產階段 ， 待2H27 台中梧棲新廠完工後加入量產 ， 總體而言 ，2026
年與 2027 年人形機器人線束出貨量預估各為 1 萬與 2 萬套，挹注總營收各為 5 億元
與 10 億元左右，目標於 2028 年挑戰 20 萬套；2)智慧交通與倉儲物流：信邦既有的

### Page 4

個股報告 
工業控制將拓展至多種場景的無人化營運 ， 包含與歐美大廠合作製造自動導引車(AGV)
與自主移動機器人(AMR)專用的高階線材 ， 另外信邦憑藉高階客製化能力也成功將技術
切入商用運輸無人機與電動垂直起降飛行器(eVTOL，俗稱空中計程車)供應鏈，提供具
備航太級安全認證的超輕量、強效抗干擾連接器模組；3)半導體設備：信邦相關產品已
從單純的線材零件延伸至高階機櫃整機組裝，旗下半導體設備客戶從過去的 2 家擴展
至目前的 6 家 ， 包含艾司摩爾(ASML.US)曝光機的超高傳輸線束等 ；4)電源與冷卻等 AI
基礎建設 ： 信邦與全球電力設備模組廠合作 ， 使CSP 大廠在集中式配線櫃(Power Rack)
與匯流排(Busbar Connector Harness) 中採用信邦的超大電流耐高壓主幹線束，另外
還積極切入資料中心中 AI 伺服器與機房的浸沒式液冷連接方案。 
投研部也預估信邦的汽車領域成長性最高， 在 2026 年與 2027 年分別為 41.4%與
23.3%，儘管公司信邦的中國車用產線減少了美系出口外銷，但全面轉向服務比亞迪等
體系的中國在地一線新能源品牌車廠， 專攻中國車廠外銷車款所需的高壓充電 、 電池管
理系統(BMS)及 ADAS 智慧駕駛感測模組線束 ； 公司在日本市場也頗有斬獲 ，自主研發
的直流快充液冷系統順利取得 UL 認證後成功打入日本大型車企 ，同時也透過台灣和中
國產線擴大對日系車廠車載影音與動力控制模組線束的直銷渗透與高度彈性支援；美
國與歐洲市場則受惠於電動車的發展而獲得訂單，信邦已完成對美國客戶 NACS(北美
充電標準)的連接器及高壓充電線束的量產準備，在 2026 年隨著當地基礎設施建設大
量出貨，而歐洲則採取積極的進攻策略，2026 年 6 月首次將電動車充電全系列產品帶
入歐洲 ， 參展慕尼黑Power2Drive Europe 2026 展會 ， 展出專為歐洲開發的歐規Type 
2 交流、CCS2 直流以及 CCS2 液冷充電線與充電插座，升級輕量化的全新設計。 
醫療照護與綠能估成長 17.3%與 11%，通訊產業則小幅衰退 0.9% 
此外，在醫療照護方面，過去佔整體交貨量約 50%的中國產線已經隨美系客戶要求而
移轉至美墨 ， 在俄亥俄州擴建規模達1.5 倍的新廠以及在墨西哥聖路易斯波托西新廠進
行美墨雙廠協作 ， 客製化藥物霧化器與醫用高壓電纜則持續獲歐系客戶積極採購 ， 再加
上供應給日本的高階客製化線束與診斷影像設備相關市佔逐漸增加， 因此預估營收
YoY+17.3%；在綠色能源方面，中國產線的綠能外銷比重在 2026 年因競爭激烈而持
續下降 ， 但日本目前存在小型綠能儲能設備需求 ， 信邦主要配合當地的能源系統整合商
供應高階儲能櫃與分散式微電網的工業級客製化連接線組，在美國墨西哥新廠與美國
俄亥俄州新廠已於近年完工，並在 2026 年步入產能全面放量期，另外公司也受惠於歐
洲風電 大廠 精簡 成本而 在 2026 年獲 得 與 Vestas (VWS.CO)、Siemens Gamesa 
(SGREN)等深化策略結盟的機會，因此預估營收 YoY+11%；在通訊領域方面，信邦正
積極爭取美國企業的低軌衛星地面接收站與陣列天線線束訂單，並針對美國大客戶提
供 AI 資料中心高頻高速傳輸線，惟 2026 年仍處於新舊產品交替期，需待 2027 年營
收才會顯著放大，歐洲通訊訂單主要集中於大型網通設備商的工業級交換機與骨幹網
路連接元件， 因此預估營收 YoY-0.9%。投研部預估 信邦 3Q26 營收 94.98 億元，
QoQ+5.3%，YoY+25.8%；毛利率 24.7%，+0.2pts. QoQ，+1.7pts. YoY；營業利益
率 10.7%，+0.3pts. QoQ，+2.4pts. YoY；稅後淨利率 10%，-0.1pts. QoQ，+0.4pts. 
YoY；稅後 EPS 3.96 元，QoQ+5.3%，YoY+30%。

/24.8%；隨著高毛利產品量產後，初期較高的研發費用將逐漸被抵銷， 營業利益率預

創意（3443 TT）

更多機會將在 2027/28 年到來

維持買進評等，目標價維持 8,000 元，110%上漲空間 
1.2Q26 每股稅後盈餘為 11.61 元，優於預期 
2.進一步成長至 2H26 
3.更多機會將在 2027/28 年到來

2Q26 每股稅後盈餘為 11.61 元，優於預期 
創意公布 2Q26 財報。營收達 139 億元 （季增21%，年增 128%） ，其中NRE/IP 為 23
億元（季增 41%，年增 11%），turnkey 為 116 億元（季增 18%，年增 188%） 。毛利率
為 21.54%（季減 5.7 個百分點 ， 年減11.8 個百分點） 。 營業費用為13 億元 （季增2%，
年增 21%） ，營業利益率為12.1%。EPS 為 11.61 元，優於我們預估的10.55 元及市場共
識的 10.26 元。財報表現超出公司指引。總營收/turnkey 營收/NRE 及其他分別季增
21%/18%/41%，相較於公司先前指引的各項單位季增個位數。

進一步成長至 2H26 
對於 3Q26， 我們預期總營收將季增低雙位數 。NRE 營收預計季減雙位數 ， 而turnkey
營收預計季增雙位數。毛利率預計略低於 1Q26 水準，但仍高於 20%。營業費用預
計略低於 2Q26 水準，因此營業利益率預計與 2Q26 持平。對於 FY26，我們預估總
營收將高於公司先前指引。NRE 營收預測將由持平年增修正為年增高雙位數，受惠
於強勁專案動能。turnkey 營收預計將較先前指引有更強勁成長。營業費用指引維持
年增雙位數不變。對於 FY27，營收預計大幅提升，營業費用成長有限，意味著營運
槓桿顯著 。 整體而言 ， 我們預估FY26/FY27 營收分別為 632 億元 （年增85%）/2060
億元（年增 226%） ，毛利率為22.2%（年減 2.6 個百分點）/17.1%（年減 5.1 個百分
點） ，EPS 分別為 60.51 元/202.14 元。

更多機會將在 2027/28 年到來 
管理層提供了一系列令人振奮的長期展望更新 ， 進一步強化了對公司以AI 為驅動的
成長軌跡的信心 。 最大亮點是管理層強烈暗示公司極有可能參與Google 下一代 TPU
運算晶片計畫，這項計畫未來幾年可能成為重要的成長催化劑。其次，供應鏈限制
似乎不再是主要擔憂，因管理層對於取得足夠晶圓分配以支援未來專案擴展表現出
高度樂觀態度 。 此外 ， 下一代CPU 與 TSLA 專案的發展路線圖依然明確 。 下一代CPU
預計於 FY26 年底設計定案，TSLA 專案的 PPA 表現領先競爭對手。最後，管理層確
認公司已與主要 CPO 客戶展開合作，進一步強化其在下一代 AI 網路基礎設施的定
位。整體而言，此次會議加深我們的正面看法，認為公司具備良好條件受益於超大
規模雲端業者 AI 投資擴大 ， 擁有多項高價值設計案與提升的獲利槓桿 ， 支持公司多
年度成長展望。

富邦不同之處 
我們對 FY26 與 FY27 EPS 的預測分別為 60.51 元與 202.14 元 ， 與市場共識的48.74 元
與 108.18 元相比，預測更高。

估值與風險 
我們維持買進評等，目標股價為 8,000 元（以 FY27 EPS 202.14 元預測乘以 40 倍本
益比計算） 。我們對FY27 EPS 的預測基本維持不變。本益比維持在 40 倍，為創意歷
史區間的低端 （40~50 倍本益比） 。 我們建議的主要風險包括產能短缺及專案進度低
於預期。

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months 
Underweight  Sector expected to underperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months.

2026/08/03 投資速報

電子–半導體 
創意 (3443.TT) 
產能爭取無虞，迎接 26-28 年爆發成長期

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部對創意的投資建議調升至強力買進，目標價上修至6080 元，
係因(1)2Q26 財報優於市場預期，(2)3Q26 動能持續上修，(3)產能
取得順利，CPU 領域有產能的廠商將受惠，加上下一代產品明年將
開始量產，故貢獻程度有所上修，(4)車用晶片雖然北美客戶 27 年
貢獻程度下修 ， 不過中國客戶補上 ，28 年展望更為樂觀 。 綜合以上 ，
上修創意 26/27 年 EPS 分別為 54.69 元(原估 52.55 元，+94.4%YOY)
及 152.4 元(原估 112.5 元，+178.8%YOY)。近期股價隨大盤大幅修
正，不過時序進入26 年下半年，創意的成長動能更加明確上修，且
費用控管十分優異，客戶從雲端 AI 延伸至終端 AI，未來更有光通
高速傳輸潛在客戶，故對創意看法更加樂觀，調升投資建議至強力
買進，目標價調升至 6080 元(2027 年EPS*40XPER)。

重點摘要： 
1. 2Q26 獲利大幅優於預期：  
2Q26 創 意 營 收 為 大 幅 優 於 預 期 的138.9 億 元 ， 成 長
21.4%QOQ，成長 127.6%YOY，其中，量產營收 115.8 億元，
成長 18%QOQ，成長188%YOY，NRE 及 IP 營收 23.1 億元，成
長 41%QOQ，成長 11%YOY，量產及營收皆大幅優於預期，量
產主要原因是在晶圓代工廠爭取展能順利 ， 使得出貨量增長
比預期各樂觀；而在 NRE 方面，也是數個客戶產品順利
SHIPOUT 所致。但毛利率下滑至 21.55%，不如原先預期的
24%，係因 SHIPOUT 的 NRE 有包含光罩及晶圓成本，加上量
產也比預期高所致 。 費用方面控管良好 ， 僅有RD 費用增加 ，
其餘皆下滑，故營業利益達到 16.7 億元，優於原先預期的
15.8 億元，稅後淨利達到15.54 億元，衰退5.5%QOQ，成長
99%YOY，EPS 11.61 元(原估 10.31 元)。

2. 3Q26 預估上修且續創新高  
展望 3Q26，雖然創意在此次法說會未給財測，不過因為先進
製程產能取得順利，故預估量產營收仍有雙位數成長，然而
NRE 推估部分在 2Q26 提前認列，故將會衰退。另外毛利率也
因為量產持續提升而下滑，不過費用將持續控管在相對低的
水位 。 綜合以上 ， 預估創意3Q26 營收為 159.2 億元(原估 154
億元)， 成長14.6%QOQ， 成長84.8%YOY， 毛利率預估為19%(原
估 22%)，不過費用控管良好，故預估稅後淨利將到15.97 億
元，成長2.7%QOQ，成長84.4%YOY，EPS 11.92 元(原估 12.9
元)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. CPU產品持續上修預估： 
研究部先前預估26年 CPU出貨量為200萬顆，但先前預估一
顆晶片為SINGLE DIE ，不過後經訪查後設計為將兩顆DIE封
裝成為一顆晶片，故換算成晶片將為 100萬顆晶片。展望26
年，目前因為X86的CPU缺貨中，故在法說會中公司提到客戶
更願意改用自研ARM BASE的CPU，故究部預估CPU出貨量上修
至120萬顆以上，貢獻營收預估上修為12億美元。展望27年，
目前CPU與該CSP ASIC配比為1:4， 將逐步提升至1:2， 故以目
前市場預估27年CSP ASIC 出貨量將達到800萬顆以上，故搭
配的CPU將至少有200萬顆(400萬顆DIE)以上。另外創意在
4Q26將有CSP 二代CPU TAPE OUT ，而二代CPU單價將略高於
一代產品 ， 故綜合以上 ， 以200萬顆一代CPU及50萬顆二代CPU
計算，將貢獻創意營收25億美元以上(先前預估為17-18億美
元)。

4. 車用晶片北美雖然下修，但中國補上：  
北美車用客戶所用的晶片雖然在今年四月底已經TAPE OUT，
但大幅量產時間點應該會落在3Q27，相對先前樂觀預估2Q27
約慢一個季度 ， 且市場有傳出先前配置為兩顆5代晶片 ， 但現
在降規為1顆4.1代晶片1顆5代晶片，故市場認為該晶片對於
創意的貢獻性有所打折。然而，在法說會中，董事長表示在
5代晶片方面 ， 創意無論TAPE OUT速度及PPA等皆遠優於對手 。
故研究部認為，若競爭對手研發順利，將不會有4.1代晶片，
所以研究員認為目前5代晶片競爭對手仍有研發瓶頸。而從
晶片應用來看，4代晶片是在21年量產，主要支援FSD功能，
故以功能來看 ，4代晶片要支援完全無人駕駛有其風險 ， 故研
究部認為未來還是會全數導入 5代晶片。因為該款晶片先前
預估27年完整貢獻 ， 目前修正為貢獻約2.5個季度 ， 營收原先
預估貢獻15億美元以上 ， 下修為12億美元 。 至於在6.5代晶片
為2nm製程 ， 與台積電配合研發服務客戶 ， 目前與對手的技術
持續拉大，故對該客戶產品的貢獻延續性非常有信心。另外
在中國客戶方面，該客戶的第 7代ADAS晶片已經導入新車使
用，將在27年逐步成為新款車用標配。研調機構數據顯示，
中國客戶在6代高階智能駕駛晶 片26年上半年出貨達到 300
萬套以上 ， 故推估7代晶片在27年搭載在新車上後 ， 保守預估
出貨量將達到百萬套以上，推估貢獻營收將達到 5億美元以
上，總計車用晶片將貢獻15億美元以上，與原預估相當。

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

圖一、TESLA 各世代 AI 晶片功能

資料來源：TESLA，X，統一研究部整理

在車用晶片未來推估方面，首先在北美客戶方面，因為北美
客戶會將該晶片用在 CYBERCAB 及 OPTIMUS 上，依照北美客
戶給的理論產能 ，27 年將僅是開端 ，28 年推估貢獻幅度將持
續大幅攀升。

圖二、TESLA 對於 CYBERCAB 及 OPTIMUS 量產規劃

資料來源：TESLA，X，統一研究部整理

另外在中國客戶方面 ， 以該客戶自行公告的資料來看 ，2 代、
3 代及 5 代晶片總共出貨超過 400 萬套，而6 代晶片在 2024
年發表後，25 年導入各品牌車廠，截至 26 年上半年，該客
戶的車用晶片總出貨量達到 1000 萬套以上。而 7 代晶片由
創意操刀設計，將在 27 年開始導入各品牌車廠，以 6 代晶
片銷售成長推估，7 代晶片將在 28 年有超過翻倍成長。

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

5. 新產品及新 CSP 潛在機會： 
(1)NEW CSP AI ASIC 
首先在新 CSP ASIC 客戶方面 ， 公司雖未明確揭露客戶 ， 不過
研究部認為憑藉公司後段設計能力，將提供 CSP 客戶另一個
降低成本的解決方案，客戶可以自行設計前段後，後段及整
合交付創意，畢竟創意具有 HBM BASE DIE 及CPO 相關的IP
及技術 ， 公司將會極力爭取 。 另外 ， 在合約負債及庫存方面 ，
公司表示有幫客戶備料 HBM 等，研究部認為CSP 客戶下一代
ASIC 都會將記憶體升級成 HBM 4，故創意幫客戶備料有可能
是為客戶下一代產品做研發而準備。從公告的合約負債可以
看出，客戶的 PREPAYMENT 持續創高。

圖三、創意各季存貨及合約負債金額

資料來源：CMONEY，統一研究部整理

(2)CPO 相關 
公司已有合作廠商，並且已經有大客戶持續 CO-WORK，公司
認為越早進入該領域機會越多。至於 CPO 相關產品有沒有機
會搭著加速器量產一起出，公司表示目前案件仍在初期，持
續與客戶合作研發。

6. 產能爭取無虞，持續上修： 
創意持續開出優於市場預期的 2Q26 財報 ， 另外展望下半年 ，
在產能爭取無虞狀況下，營收看法相對先前樂觀。雖然毛利
率因為產品組合變化而下修，不過費用控管相當優異，故獲
利也同步上修。上修創意 26 年營收為 648.6 億元(原估 594
億元)，成長 90%YOY，稅後淨利同步上修預估為 73.28 億元

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

(原估 70.4 億元)， 成長94.4%YOY，EPS 54.69 元(原估 52.55
元)。展望 27 年，CPU 貢獻性持續上修，另外北美車用客戶
雖然貢獻性下修，但中國客戶補上，另外本來就有 CSP 加速
器開始邁入量產，故上修 27 年營收預估為 1606 億元(原估
1263 億元)， 成長147.6%YOY， 稅後淨利上修預估為204.3 億
元(原估 150.7 億元)，成長 178.8%YOY，EPS 152.4 元(原估
112.5 元)。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上

供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之
著 作 權 ， 嚴 禁 抄 襲 與 仿 造 。

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部維持立積的投資建議為買進 ， 但下修目標價 ，係因(1)2Q26
營收雖然回升但不如預期 ， 需求仍受到成本高漲壓抑 ，(2)3Q26 部
分產品開始反映漲價，下半年動能優於上半年，(3)WIFI 7 在 26
年貢獻營收仍將翻倍成長，(4)27 年有望重回成長。下修立積 26
年 EPS 預估為 3.06 元(原估 5.31 元，+15.1%YOY)，對27 年以較
保守方式預估，EPS 推估為 4.47 元(+45.9%YOY)。在投資建議上，
雖然 DDR 4 單價高漲，使得客戶訂單能見度較低，另外供應鏈漲
價也使獲利倍受侵蝕 ， 故研究部也下修26 年獲利 ， 不過考量WIFI 
7 升級趨勢不變，確實在26 年營收翻倍成長，谷底已過，下半年
動能回升，故研究部維持買進建議，但目標價下修至 115 元
(25XPER*2027 年 EPS)。

重點摘要： 
1. 2Q26雖走出谷底，但整體需求仍受到成本高漲壓抑： 
立積2Q26營收僅9.77億元，不如原先預期的10.7億元，成
長5.1%QOQ，成長0.3%YOY，主還是還客戶端取得短料貨源
較難 ， 故長料拉貨動能較緩。 毛利率提升至34%， 相對於先
前預估35%仍較低 ， 就是在晶圓代工跟封裝上面成本提升 ，
立積將逐步反映。費用2.89億元，OPEX RATIO降低至30%。
但因為營收規模較低，加上毛利率未大幅提升下，且業外
在匯率方面有所虧損，故稅後淨利僅為 3800萬元，衰退
27.3%QOQ，YOY轉盈 ，EPS 0.42元 ， 不如原先預期的1.06元。

2. 3Q26未給財測，但給出下半年優於上半年的想法：  
雖然公司未給明確3Q26展望，但有表現下半年將優於上半
年 ， 且3Q26多項新專案持續進行中 ， 但供應鏈影響仍存在 ，
記憶體及部分被動元件會有影響，但交貨狀況跟售價已經
有部分開始提升。但研究部先前預期較為樂觀，故仍下修
3Q26 營收預估為 10.2 億元 ( 原估 12.45 億元 ) ， 成 長
4.4%QOQ， 成長3.5%YOY，EPS也下修預估至1.06元(原估1.8
元)。

3. 產品有逐步反映成本，也確實仍持續升級： 
在立積本身方面，部分產品是砷化鎵代工，但部分也是
矽製程晶圓代工，故目前供應鏈整體處於漲價狀態，包
括封裝廠，公司主要的目標仍是確保產能，成本會適當
反映給客戶。另外，在2Q26 WIFI各世代產品營收來看，

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

即使在成本高漲狀況下，WIFI 7產生的營收仍持續攀
升，故研究部認為，在成本能逐步反映出去，加上WIFI 
7在產業逆風的狀況下仍持續攀升，推估毛利率雖然相對
先前預估低，但仍能逐步攀升。

4. WIFI 7 26年貢獻營收確實翻倍，但其餘下滑，27年持續成
長： 
研究部先前對立積看法較為樂觀，但 1Q26確實是谷底，
WIFI 7持續成長，不過等級較低的WIFI 6因為終端受到記
憶體漲價提升下，在2Q26出貨大幅下滑。下半年雖然比上
半年好，不過觀察到多數零組件價格仍未回落，立積為相
對是長料的供應商，故下修26年營收預估為39.43億元(原
估44.82億元)，成長4.8%YOY，稅後淨利同步下修預估為
2.82億元(原估4.9億元)，成長15.1%YOY，EPS 3.06元(原
估5.31元)。展望27年，WIFI 8即將問世，另外26年遞延的
WIFI 7需求將逐步出現，推估營收將緩步回升，以較保守
的情境推估，27年營收回升至44.33億元，成長12.4%YOY，
稅後淨利預估為4.12億元，成長45.9%YOY，EPS 4.47元。

資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之
著 作 權 ， 嚴 禁 抄 襲 與 仿 造 。
